42321: // === High Scores (Match) — v4: movement ▲/▼ (7d), NEW(7d), PB, date fmt =====
42322: (function enhanceHighScoresMatch_v4(){
42323:   const TITLE_RX = /^\s*high\s*scores\s*$/i;
42324:   // Expected header: "# | Player | Score | Avg / Round | When"
42325:   const COLS_RX  = [/^#$/i, /player/i, /^score$/i, /avg\s*\/\s*round/i, /when/i];
42326:   const NEW_MS   = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 7 days
42327:   const POS_KEY  = 'hsMatchEntryPositions.v1'; // entryKey -> {pos, delta, tISO}
42328: 
42329:   // ---------- helpers ----------
42330:   function findModalAndTable(){
42331:     const mods = document.querySelectorAll('.modal,[role="dialog"]');
42332:     for (const m of mods){
42333:       const ok = Array.from(m.querySelectorAll('h1,h2,h3'))
42334:         .some(h => TITLE_RX.test(h.textContent||''));
42335:       if (!ok) continue;
42336:       const table = m.querySelector('table.hs-table'); if (!table) continue;
42337:       const ths = Array.from(table.querySelectorAll('thead th'));
42338:       if (ths.length < 5) continue;
42339:       const matches = COLS_RX.every((rx, idx)=> rx.test((ths[idx].textContent||'').trim()));
42340:       if (matches) return { modal: m, table, ths };
42341:     }
42342:     return null;
42343:   }
42344: 
42345:   // "20 Nov 25 at 20:35" | "20 Nov 25 @ 20:35" → "20/11/25 @ 20:35"
42346:   function fmt_DDMMYY_HHMM(text){
42347:     if (!text) return text;
42348:     const m = text.match(/^\s*(\d{1,2})\s+([A-Za-z]{3})\s+(\d{2})(?:\s+at|\s*@)\s+(\d{2}):(\d{2})\s*$/);
42349:     if (!m) return text;
42350:     const [, d, mon, yy, hh, mm] = m;
42351:     const MONTH = {JAN:'01',FEB:'02',MAR:'03',APR:'04',MAY:'05',JUN:'06',JUL:'07',AUG:'08',SEP:'09',OCT:'10',NOV:'11',DEC:'12'};
42352:     const mmNum = MONTH[mon.toUpperCase()];
42353:     if (!mmNum) return text;
42354:     const dd = String(d).padStart(2,'0');
42355:     return `${dd}/${mmNum}/${yy} @ ${hh}:${mm}`;
42356:   }
42357: 
42358:   function yellowNEW(){
42359:     const span = document.createElement('span');
42360:     span.className = 'tag';
42361:     span.textContent = 'NEW';
42362:     span.style.color = '#facc15';
42363:     span.style.borderColor = 'rgba(250,204,21,.35)';
42364:     span.style.background = 'rgba(250,204,21,.08)';
42365:     return span;
42366:   }
42367: 
42368:   function arrowHTML(delta){
42369:     if (delta > 0) return `<span title="+${delta}" style="color:#22c55e;font-weight:700">▲</span>`;
42370:     if (delta < 0) return `<span title="${delta}" style="color:#ef4444;font-weight:700">▼</span>`;
42371:     return `<span style="opacity:.5">–</span>`;
42372:   }
42373: 
42374:   // Build PB map + (player|score)→latest timestamp
42375:   async function buildMaps(){
42376:     if (window.__hsMatchMaps_v4) return window.__hsMatchMaps_v4;
42377: 
42378:     let games = [];
42379:     try { games = (await __fetchOfficialGames(50000)).map(__normalizeGame); } catch(_) {}
42380: 
42381:     const pb = new Map();      // nameLC -> max score
42382:     const keyTs = new Map();   // `${nameLC}|${score}` -> latest Date
42383:     let globalMax = 0;
42384: 
42385:     games.forEach(g=>{
42386:       const ts = __gameTs(g.raw || g);
42387:       const d  = ts ? new Date(ts) : null;
42388:       (g.players||[]).forEach((p,i)=>{
42389:         const name = String(p||'').trim(); if (!name) return;
42390:         const k = name.toLowerCase();
42391:         const score = Number(g.totals?.[i] || 0);
42392:         if (!Number.isFinite(score)) return;
42393:         if (score > (pb.get(k) || 0)) pb.set(k, score);
42394:         if (score > globalMax) globalMax = score;
42395:         if (d) {
42396:           const key = `${k}|${score}`;
42397:           const prev = keyTs.get(key);
42398:           if (!prev || d > prev) keyTs.set(key, d);
42399:         }
42400:       });
42401:     });
42402: 
42403:     window.__hsMatchMaps_v4 = { pb, keyTs, globalMax };
42404:     return window.__hsMatchMaps_v4;
42405:   }
42406: 
42407:   function loadPos(){ try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(POS_KEY) || '{}'); } catch(_) { return {}; } }
42408:   function savePos(m){ try { localStorage.setItem(POS_KEY, JSON.stringify(m)); } catch(_){} }
42409: 
42410:   async function apply(){
42411:     const found = findModalAndTable(); if (!found) return;
42412:     const { table, ths } = found;
42413: 
42414:     // Insert movement header AFTER "#"
42415:     let thMove = table.querySelector('thead th[data-move-col="1"]');
42416:     if (!thMove){
42417:       thMove = document.createElement('th');
42418:       thMove.dataset.moveCol = '1';
42419:       thMove.style.textAlign = 'center';
42420:       thMove.style.width     = '26px';
42421:       thMove.style.minWidth  = '22px';
42422:       thMove.style.maxWidth  = '34px';
42423:       ths[0].insertAdjacentElement('afterend', thMove);
42424:     }
42425: 
42426:     // Insert NEW header AFTER movement
42427:     let headNow = Array.from(table.querySelectorAll('thead th'));
42428:     let idxRank = headNow.findIndex(th => /^#$/i.test((th.textContent||'').trim()));
42429:     let idxMove = headNow.findIndex(th => th.dataset && th.dataset.moveCol === '1');
42430: 
42431:     let thNew = table.querySelector('thead th[data-newcol="1"]');
42432:     if (!thNew){
42433:       thNew = document.createElement('th');
42434:       thNew.dataset.newcol = '1';
42435:       thNew.style.textAlign = 'center';
42436:       thNew.style.width = '44px';
42437:       thNew.style.minWidth = '38px';
42438:       thNew.style.maxWidth = '60px';
42439:       headNow[idxMove].insertAdjacentElement('afterend', thNew);
42440:     }
42441: 
42442:     // Narrow the "#" column
42443:     headNow = Array.from(table.querySelectorAll('thead th'));
42444:     idxRank = headNow.findIndex(th => /^#$/i.test((th.textContent||'').trim()));
42445:     idxMove = headNow.findIndex(th => th.dataset && th.dataset.moveCol === '1');
42446:     const idxNew = headNow.findIndex(th => th.dataset && th.dataset.newcol === '1');
42447: 
42448:     if (headNow[idxRank]) {
42449:       headNow[idxRank].style.width     = '24px';
42450:       headNow[idxRank].style.minWidth  = '22px';
42451:       headNow[idxRank].style.maxWidth  = '28px';
42452:       headNow[idxRank].style.textAlign = 'center';
42453:     }
42454:     table.style.tableLayout = 'fixed';
42455:     table.style.width = '100%';
42456: 
42457:     // Resolve main column indexes by header text (after our inserts)
42458:     const idxPlayer = headNow.findIndex(th => /player/i.test(th.textContent||''));
42459:     const idxScore  = headNow.findIndex(th => /^score$/i.test(th.textContent||''));
42460:     let   idxWhen   = headNow.findIndex(th => /when/i.test(th.textContent||''));
42461:     if (idxWhen < 0) idxWhen = headNow.length - 1;
42462: 
42463:     const { pb, keyTs, globalMax } = await buildMaps();
42464: 
42465:     // Load previous entry positions (per entry: nameLC|score)
42466:     const prev = loadPos();
42467:     const next = {};
42468:     const now = Date.now();
42469: 
42470:     // Per-row updates
42471:     const rows = Array.from(table.querySelectorAll('tbody tr'));
42472:     rows.forEach((tr, i)=>{
42473:       const tds0 = Array.from(tr.children);
42474:       const tdRank = tds0[idxRank];
42475: 
42476:       // Ensure movement cell immediately after rank
42477:       let tdMove = tds0[idxMove];
42478:       if (!tdMove || tdMove === tdRank || tdMove.dataset.moveCell !== '1') {
42479:         tdMove = document.createElement('td');
42480:         tdMove.dataset.moveCell = '1';
42481:         tdMove.style.textAlign = 'center';
42482:         tdRank.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', tdMove);
42483:       } else {
42484:         tdMove.innerHTML = '';
42485:       }
42486: 
42487:       // Ensure NEW cell immediately after movement
42488:       const tds1 = Array.from(tr.children);
42489:       const tdAfterMove = tds1[idxMove + 1] || tdMove.nextElementSibling;
42490:       let tdNew = tds1[idxNew];
42491:       if (!tdNew || tdNew === tdRank || tdNew === tdMove || tdNew.dataset.newCell !== '1') {
42492:         tdNew = document.createElement('td');
42493:         tdNew.dataset.newCell = '1';
42494:         tdNew.style.textAlign = 'center';
42495:         (tdAfterMove || tdMove).insertAdjacentElement('afterend', tdNew);
42496:       } else {
42497:         tdNew.innerHTML = '';
42498:       }
42499: 
42500:       // Refresh indexes after inserts and read data cells
42501:       const tds = Array.from(tr.children);
42502:       const tdPlayer = tds[idxPlayer];
42503:       const tdScore  = tds[idxScore];
42504:       const tdWhen   = tds[idxWhen] || tds[tds.length - 1];
42505: 
42506:       // 1) Format When => DD/MM/YY @ HH:MM
42507:       if (tdWhen) {
42508:         const orig = tdWhen.textContent || '';
42509:         const fmt  = fmt_DDMMYY_HHMM(orig);
42510:         if (fmt && fmt !== orig) tdWhen.textContent = fmt;
42511:       }
42512: 
42513:       // Entry key for movement & NEW: nameLC|score
42514:       const nameLC = (tdPlayer?.textContent || '').trim().toLowerCase();
42515:       const scoreN = Number((tdScore?.textContent || '').replace(/[^\d.]/g,''));
42516:       const entryKey = `${nameLC}|${Number.isFinite(scoreN) ? scoreN : -1}`;
42517:       const pos = i + 1;
42518: 
42519:       // 2) Movement (persist 7 days if no further movement)
42520:       const prevRec = prev[entryKey];
42521:       let deltaToShow = 0;
42522:       if (prevRec && typeof prevRec.pos === 'number') {
42523:         if (prevRec.pos !== pos) {
42524:           // changed now
42525:           deltaToShow = prevRec.pos - pos; // + = moved up
42526:           next[entryKey] = { pos, delta: deltaToShow, tISO: new Date(now).toISOString() };
42527:         } else {
42528:           // unchanged; keep showing if within 7d and delta != 0
42529:           const ageOK = prevRec.tISO && (now - Date.parse(prevRec.tISO)) <= NEW_MS;
42530:           deltaToShow = ageOK ? (prevRec.delta || 0) : 0;
42531:           next[entryKey] = { pos, delta: prevRec.delta || 0, tISO: prevRec.tISO || new Date(now).toISOString() };
42532:         }
42533:       } else {
42534:         // first time seeing this entry
42535:         next[entryKey] = { pos, delta: 0, tISO: new Date(now).toISOString() };
42536:       }
42537:       tdMove.innerHTML = arrowHTML(deltaToShow);
42538: 
42539:       // 3) NEW (≤7 days since that exact (player,score))
42540:       if (nameLC && Number.isFinite(scoreN)) {
42541:         const k = `${nameLC}|${scoreN}`;
42542:         const ts = keyTs.get(k);
42543:         if (ts && (Date.now() - ts.getTime()) <= NEW_MS) {
42544:           tdNew.appendChild(yellowNEW());
42545:         }
42546:       }
42547: 
42548:       // 4) PB after Score — show ONLY ONCE per player (true PB occurrence), exclude global top score
42549:       const isGlobalTop = Number((tdScore?.textContent || '').replace(/[^\d.]/g,'')) === globalMax;
42550:       const best = pb.get(nameLC) || -Infinity;
42551:       const curScore = Number((tdScore?.textContent || '').replace(/[^\d.]/g,''));
42552:       const isPBScore = Number.isFinite(curScore) && curScore === best;
42553: 
42554:       // Prefer tagging the latest occurrence of that PB score (so PB doesn't repeat across multiple equal-score rows)
42555:       function parseWhenMs(txt){
42556:         if (!txt) return NaN;
42557:         // Expect "DD/MM/YY @ HH:MM"
42558:         const m = String(txt).trim().match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\s*@\s*(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
42559:         if (!m) return NaN;
42560:         const dd = Number(m[1]), mm = Number(m[2]), yy = Number(m[3]), hh = Number(m[4]), mi = Number(m[5]);
42561:         if (![dd,mm,yy,hh,mi].every(Number.isFinite)) return NaN;
42562:         // Stored timestamps are UTC; treat display as UTC for matching.
42563:         return Date.UTC(2000 + yy, mm - 1, dd, hh, mi, 0, 0);
42564:       }
42565: 
42566:       const pbKey = `${nameLC}|${best}`;
42567:       const latestPbTs = keyTs.get(pbKey) || null; // Date (latest occurrence from official games)
42568:       const rowWhenMs = parseWhenMs(tdWhen?.textContent || '');
42569:       const matchLatest = (latestPbTs && Number.isFinite(rowWhenMs))
42570:         ? Math.abs(latestPbTs.getTime() - rowWhenMs) <= 60 * 1000
42571:         : true; // if we can't parse, don't block tagging
42572: 
42573:       if (!isGlobalTop && isPBScore && matchLatest && tdScore && !seenPB.has(nameLC) && !/\bPB\b/i.test(tdScore.innerHTML)) {
42574:         const tag = document.createElement('span');
42575:         tag.className = 'tag';
42576:         tag.textContent = 'PB';
42577:         tag.style.marginLeft = '6px';
42578:         tdScore.appendChild(tag);
42579:         seenPB.add(nameLC);
42580:       }
42581:     });
42582: 
42583:     // Save snapshot for next time
42584:     savePos(next);
42585:   }
42586: 
42587:   function tick(){ try { apply(); } catch(e){ console.error('HS Match v4 failed', e); } }
42588:   if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', tick); else tick();
42589:   try { window.__sqUIMutationBus?.on(()=>tick()); } catch(_) {}
42590: })();

42866: // === In-game minis ( (xx) ) — strip green boxes; only highest is green ======
42867: (function strictBracketWinner(){
42868:   const GREEN = '#6ee7b7';
42869:   const MUTED = 'rgba(255,255,255,.6)';
42870:   const RX = /^\(\d{1,3}\)$/; // "(30)", "(99)", etc.
42871: 
42872:   function isMini(el){
42873:     if (!el || !el.offsetParent) return false;
42874:     const t = (el.textContent || '').trim();
42875:     return RX.test(t);
42876:   }
42877: 
42878:   // remove any .tag styling that makes them boxed/green
42879:   function sanitize(el){
42880:     // If the mini or its parent uses .tag for the bracket, strip it
42881:     const nodes = [el, el.parentElement];
42882:     for (const n of nodes){
42883:       if (!n) continue;
42884:       if (n.classList && n.classList.contains('tag') && RX.test((n.textContent||'').trim())){
42885:         n.classList.remove('tag');
42886:         // clear visual remnants
42887:         n.style.removeProperty('background');
42888:         n.style.removeProperty('border');
42889:         n.style.removeProperty('borderColor');
42890:         n.style.removeProperty('color');
42891:         n.style.removeProperty('box-shadow');
42892:       }
42893:     }
42894:   }
42895: 
42896:   // find minis within a row-like container
42897:   function findRowMinis(row){
42898:     // pick visible nodes that look like a mini
42899:     const cand = Array.from(row.querySelectorAll('small,span,strong,div'));
42900:     const minis = cand.filter(isMini);
42901:     if (minis.length < 2) return [];
42902: 
42903:     // sort by x, then take the left-most and right-most (the two player columns)
42904:     minis.sort((a,b)=>a.getBoundingClientRect().left - b.getBoundingClientRect().left);
42905:     return [minis[0], minis[minis.length-1]];
42906:   }
42907: 
42908:   function valueOf(el){
42909:     return parseInt((el.textContent||'').replace(/[^\d]/g,''), 10) || 0;
42910:   }
42911: 
42912:   function paintRow(miniL, miniR){
42913:     if (!miniL || !miniR) return;
42914:     sanitize(miniL); sanitize(miniR);
42915: 
42916:     const vL = valueOf(miniL);
42917:     const vR = valueOf(miniR);
42918: 
42919:     if (vL > vR){
42920:       miniL.style.color = GREEN;
42921:       miniR.style.color = MUTED;
42922:     } else if (vR > vL){
42923:       miniL.style.color = MUTED;
42924:       miniR.style.color = GREEN;
42925:     } else {
42926:       // tie -> neither green
42927:       miniL.style.color = MUTED;
42928:       miniR.style.color = MUTED;
42929:     }
42930:   }
42931: 
42932:   function apply(root){
42933:     // Try table rows first
42934:     const rows = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('table tr,[role="row"]'))
42935:       .filter(r => r.offsetParent);
42936:     let touched = 0;
42937:     rows.forEach(r=>{
42938:       const [a,b] = findRowMinis(r);
42939:       if (a && b){ paintRow(a,b); touched++; }
42940:     });
42941: 
42942:     // Fallback: scan visible sections that look like round rows
42943:     if (!touched){
42944:       const sections = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('section,div,li'))
42945:         .filter(s => s.offsetParent);
42946:       sections.forEach(s=>{
42947:         const [a,b] = findRowMinis(s);
42948:         if (a && b) paintRow(a,b);
42949:       });
42950:     }
42951:   }

52722:   function pointsTargetKey(r){
52723:     var n = Number(typeof __sqTargetPctValue === 'function' ? __sqTargetPctValue(r, ['target_number','target_n','target']) : r.target_number);
52724:     if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 10 && n <= 20) return String(Math.trunc(n));
52725:     var raw = typeof __sqTargetPctValue === 'function' ? __sqTargetPctValue(r, ['target_label','target_key','bucket_key','target']) : (r.target_label || r.target_key || r.bucket_key || r.target);
52726:     var key = String(raw || '').trim().toUpperCase();
52727:     if (key === 'DOUBLE' || key === 'DOUBLES') key = 'D';
52728:     if (key === 'TREBLE' || key === 'TRIPLE' || key === 'TREBLES' || key === 'TRIPLES') key = 'T';
52729:     if (key === 'BULL' || key === 'BULLS' || key === 'IB' || key === 'OB') key = 'B';
52730:     return key;
52731:   }
52732:   function fmtAvgScore(v){
52733:     var n = Number(v);
52734:     if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return '—';
52735:     var rounded = Math.round(n * 10) / 10;
52736:     return (Number.isInteger(rounded) ? String(rounded) : rounded.toFixed(1)) + ' pts';
52737:   }
52738:   function rowFromPointsPct(r){
52739:     var targetNumber = Number(typeof __sqTargetPctValue === 'function' ? __sqTargetPctValue(r, ['target_number','target_n','target']) : r.target_number);
52740:     var key = pointsTargetKey(r);
52741:     var throwsN = Number((typeof __sqTargetPctValue === 'function' ? __sqTargetPctValue(r, ['throws','throw_count','thrown']) : r.throws) || 0);
52742:     var actual = Number((typeof __sqTargetPctValue === 'function' ? __sqTargetPctValue(r, ['actual_points','points','score','round_total']) : r.actual_points) || 0);
52743:     var max = Number((typeof __sqTargetPctValue === 'function' ? __sqTargetPctValue(r, ['max_points','available_points']) : r.max_points) || 0);
52744:     if (!Number.isFinite(max) || max <= 0) max = Number.isFinite(targetNumber) ? targetNumber * 3 * throwsN : 0;
52745:     var sourceGames = Number((typeof __sqTargetPctValue === 'function' ? __sqTargetPctValue(r, ['source_games','games','game_count']) : r.source_games) || 0);
52746:     var pctRaw = typeof __sqTargetPctValue === 'function' ? __sqTargetPctValue(r, ['points_pct','pct','percentage']) : r.points_pct;
52747:     var pct = Number.isFinite(Number(pctRaw)) ? Number(pctRaw) : (max ? actual * 100 / max : 0);
52748:     var sort = Number.isFinite(targetNumber) ? targetNumber : (typeof __sqTargetPctOrder === 'function' ? __sqTargetPctOrder(key) : 999);
52749:     var avgScore = sourceGames > 0 ? actual / sourceGames : (throwsN ? actual / (throwsN / 3) : NaN);
52750:     return {
52751:       key:key,
52752:       playerKey:rankKey(r.player_key || r.player_name),
52753:       playerName:String(r.player_name || r.player_key || ''),
52754:       targetNumber:Number.isFinite(targetNumber) ? targetNumber : null,
52755:       throwsN:throwsN,
52756:       actual:actual,
52757:       max:max,
52758:       pct:Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(pct) || 0)),
52759:       sourceGames:sourceGames,
52760:       avgScore:avgScore,
52761:       sort:sort
52762:     };
52763:   }
52764:   function applyTargetPointsPersonalRanks(rows){
52765:     var sorted = (rows || []).slice().sort(function(a,b){
52766:       return Number(b.pct || 0) - Number(a.pct || 0) ||
52767:         Number(b.actual || 0) - Number(a.actual || 0) ||
52768:         Number(a.sort || 999) - Number(b.sort || 999);
52769:     });
52770:     var rank = 0, prev = '';
52771:     sorted.forEach(function(r, i){
52772:       var sig = [Number(r.pct || 0).toFixed(8), Number(r.actual || 0), Number(r.sort || 999)].join('|');
52773:       if (sig !== prev){ rank = i + 1; prev = sig; }
52774:       r.pRank = rank;
52775:     });
52776:   }
52777:   async function fetchTargetPointsGlobalRanks(rows){
52778:     var c = client();
52779:     if (!c || typeof c.from !== 'function') return new Map();
52780:     var keys = new Set((rows || []).map(function(r){ return String(r.key || '').toUpperCase(); }).filter(Boolean));
52781:     if (!keys.size) return new Map();
52782:     try{
52783:       var savedSet = await savedPlayerKeySet();
52784:       var q = await c.from('v_player_target_points_pct')
52785:         .select('player_key,player_name,target_number,target_label,throws,actual_points,max_points,points_pct,source_games')
52786:         .limit(10000);
52787:       if (q.error) throw q.error;
52788:       var allRows = (Array.isArray(q.data) ? q.data : []).map(rowFromPointsPct).filter(function(r){
52789:         if (!r.key || !r.playerKey || !keys.has(String(r.key).toUpperCase())) return false;
52790:         return !savedSet || savedSet.has(r.playerKey);
52791:       });
52792:       var byTarget = new Map();
52793:       allRows.forEach(function(r){
52794:         var k = String(r.key).toUpperCase();
52795:         if (!byTarget.has(k)) byTarget.set(k, []);
52796:         byTarget.get(k).push(r);
52797:       });
52798:       var ranks = new Map();
52799:       byTarget.forEach(function(list, targetKey){
52800:         list.sort(function(a,b){
52801:           return Number(b.pct || 0) - Number(a.pct || 0) ||
52802:             Number(b.actual || 0) - Number(a.actual || 0) ||
52803:             Number(b.sourceGames || 0) - Number(a.sourceGames || 0) ||
52804:             String(a.playerName || a.playerKey || '').localeCompare(String(b.playerName || b.playerKey || ''));
52805:         });
52806:         var rank = 0, prev = '';
52807:         list.forEach(function(r, i){
52808:           var sig = [Number(r.pct || 0).toFixed(8), Number(r.actual || 0), Number(r.sourceGames || 0), String(r.playerName || r.playerKey || '')].join('|');
52809:           if (sig !== prev){ rank = i + 1; prev = sig; }
52810:           ranks.set(targetKey + '|' + r.playerKey, rank);
52811:         });
52812:       });
52813:       console.debug('[SQ] Target Points % global rank source used', { source:'v_player_target_points_pct', rows:allRows.length, saved_players_only:!!savedSet });
52814:       return ranks;
52815:     }catch(e){
52816:       console.warn('[SQ] Target Points % global rank source unavailable', { required_view:'v_player_target_points_pct', error:e });
52817:       return new Map();
52818:     }
52819:   }

53197:   function low(v){ return String(v == null ? '' : v).trim().toLowerCase(); }
53198:   function num(v){ var n = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null; }
53199:   function pickState(g){
53200:     if (!g) return {};
53201:     if (g.state && typeof g.state === 'object') return g.state;
53202:     if (g.raw && g.raw.state && typeof g.raw.state === 'object') return g.raw.state;
53203:     return {};
53204:   }
53205:   function pickRawState(g){
53206:     return (g && g.raw && g.raw.state && typeof g.raw.state === 'object') ? g.raw.state : {};
53207:   }
53208:   function playersOf(g){
53209:     var st = pickState(g), raw = pickRawState(g);
53210:     var ps = (g && (g.players || g.player_names)) || st.players || raw.players;
53211:     return asArr(ps);
53212:   }
53213:   function boardOf(g){
53214:     var st = pickState(g), raw = pickRawState(g);
53215:     return (g && (g.board || g.score)) || st.board || st.score || raw.board || raw.score || [];
53216:   }
53217:   function rowsForPlayer(board, idx){
53218:     if (!Array.isArray(board)) return [];
53219:     if (Array.isArray(board[idx])) return board[idx];
53220:     if (Array.isArray(board[0]) && board[0] && board[0][idx] != null){
53221:       return board.map(function(r){ return Array.isArray(r) ? r[idx] : null; });
53222:     }
53223:     return [];
53224:   }
53225:   function roundHasPlayed(ent){
53226:     if (ent == null) return false;
53227:     if (typeof ent === 'number') return true;
53228:     if (typeof ent === 'string') return ent.trim() !== '';
53229:     if (Array.isArray(ent)) return ent.length > 0;
53230:     if (typeof ent !== 'object') return false;
53231:     var darts = Array.isArray(ent.darts) ? ent.darts : (Array.isArray(ent.throws) ? ent.throws : null);
53232:     if (darts) return darts.some(function(d){ return d != null; });
53233:     var scoreKeys = ['roundTotal','round_total','points','score','total','val','value'];
53234:     for (var i=0; i<scoreKeys.length; i++){
53235:       if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(ent, scoreKeys[i])) {
53236:         var n = Number(ent[scoreKeys[i]]);
53237:         if (Number.isFinite(n) && n !== 0) return true;
53238:       }
53239:     }
53240:     return Object.keys(ent).some(function(k){
53241:       if (k === 'darts' || k === 'throws' || scoreKeys.indexOf(k) >= 0) return false;
53242:       return ent[k] != null && ent[k] !== '';
53243:     });
53244:   }
53245:   function gameLooksLikeLegacyTurboBoard(g){
53246:     try{
53247:       var board = boardOf(g);
53248:       var players = playersOf(g);
53249:       var count = Math.max(players.length || 0, Array.isArray(board) ? board.length : 0);
53250:       if (!Array.isArray(board) || count < 1) return false;
53251:       var earlyPlayed = false, latePlayed = false;
53252:       for (var pi=0; pi<count; pi++){
53253:         var rows = rowsForPlayer(board, pi);
53254:         for (var ri=0; ri<7; ri++) if (roundHasPlayed(rows[ri])) earlyPlayed = true;
53255:         for (var rj=7; rj<14; rj++) if (roundHasPlayed(rows[rj])) latePlayed = true;
53256:       }
53257:       return latePlayed && !earlyPlayed;
53258:     }catch(_){ return false; }
53259:   }
53260: 	  function explicitTurbo(g){
53261: 	    try{
53262: 	      var st = pickState(g), raw = pickRawState(g);
53263: 	      var isLiveState = !!(g && typeof window !== 'undefined' && (g === window.state || (typeof state !== 'undefined' && g === state)));
53264: 	      var draft = (g && g.__sqTournamentDraft) || st.__sqTournamentDraft || (isLiveState && window.__sqTournamentDraft) || {};
53265: 	      var match = (g && g.match) || st.match || raw.match || {};
53266:       var rules = (g && g.tournamentRules) || st.tournamentRules || match.tournamentRules || draft.rules || raw.tournamentRules || {};
53267:       var modes = [
53268:         g && g.mode, g && g.gameMode, g && g.game_mode, g && g.type,
53269:         st.mode, st.gameMode, st.game_mode, st.type,
53270:         raw.mode, raw.gameMode, raw.game_mode, raw.type,
53271:         g && g.tournamentType, g && g.tournament_type,
53272:         st.tournamentType, st.tournament_type,
53273:         match.tournamentType, match.tournament_type,
53274:         raw.tournamentType, raw.tournament_type,
53275:         draft.type
53276:       ];
53277:       if (modes.some(function(v){ return low(v) === 'turbo'; })) return true;
53278:       if (st.strictTimer === true || match.strictTimer === true || rules.strictTimer === true || raw.strictTimer === true) return true;
53279:       if (num(st.throwLimitSeconds) === 20 || num(match.throwLimitSeconds) === 20 || num(rules.throwLimitSeconds) === 20 || num(raw.throwLimitSeconds) === 20) return true;
53280:       if (low(st.startTarget) === '17' || low(match.startTarget) === '17' || low(rules.startTarget) === '17' || low(raw.startTarget) === '17') return true;
53281:       return false;
53282:     }catch(_){ return false; }
53283:   }
53284:   function explicitPractice(g){

53289:       if (mode.indexOf('practice') >= 0 || mode.indexOf('unofficial') >= 0 || mode === 'solo' || mode === 'classic') return true;
53290:       if ((g && (g.is_practice === true || g.isPractice === true || g.practice === true || g.single_player === true)) ||

53294:       return false;
53295:     }catch(_){ return false; }
53296:   }
53297:   function isArchived(g){

53300:   }
53301:   function gameModeKey(g){
53302:     if (!g || isArchived(g)) return 'archived';

53305:     if (playersOf(g).length >= 2) return 'official';
53306:     return 'unknown';

53398:   function rowMatchesCleanMode(row, mode, modeMap){

53407:   window.__sqFetchPowerRowsFromCleanRoundScores = async function(mode){

53420:       var name = String(r.player || '').trim();

53438:           String(b.gameId).localeCompare(String(a.gameId)) ||
53439:           (Number(b.roundIndex || 0) - Number(a.roundIndex || 0));

53445:       var avg = rounds ? (total / rounds) : 0;

53448:         playerKey:g.playerKey,
53449:         rounds:rounds,
53450:         powerRank:avg,
53451:         avgRound:avg,
53452:         rankPos:null,

53460:       return (b.powerRank - a.powerRank) ||
53461:         (b.rounds - a.rounds) ||
53462:         String(a.player).localeCompare(String(b.player));
53463:     });
53464:     var rank = 0;
53465:     rows.forEach(function(r){
53466:       rank += 1;
53467:       r.rankPos = rank;
53468:     });
53469:     return rows;

53899:     function pwCount(el, target, dec, dur){
53900:       if (reduced || !Number.isFinite(target)){ el.textContent = Number(target||0).toFixed(dec); return; }
53901:       var t0 = performance.now();
53902:       (function step(t){
53903:         var p = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / dur);
53904:         el.textContent = (target * (1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3))).toFixed(dec);
53905:         if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
53906:       })(t0);
53907:     }
53908:     function parseLatest(ls){
53909:       try{
53910:         if (!ls) return [];
53911:         var a = Array.isArray(ls) ? ls : (typeof ls === 'string' ? (ls.trim().charAt(0) === '[' ? JSON.parse(ls) : ls.split(/[,;\/]/)) : []);
53912:         return a.map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite).slice(0, 4);

52795:         if (!byTarget.has(k)) byTarget.set(k, []);
52796:         byTarget.get(k).push(r);
52797:       });
52798:       var ranks = new Map();
52799:       byTarget.forEach(function(list, targetKey){
52800:         list.sort(function(a,b){
52801:           return Number(b.pct || 0) - Number(a.pct || 0) ||
52802:             Number(b.actual || 0) - Number(a.actual || 0) ||
52803:             Number(b.sourceGames || 0) - Number(a.sourceGames || 0) ||
52804:             String(a.playerName || a.playerKey || '').localeCompare(String(b.playerName || b.playerKey || ''));
52805:         });
52806:         var rank = 0, prev = '';
52807:         list.forEach(function(r, i){
52808:           var sig = [Number(r.pct || 0).toFixed(8), Number(r.actual || 0), Number(r.sourceGames || 0), String(r.playerName || r.playerKey || '')].join('|');
52809:           if (sig !== prev){ rank = i + 1; prev = sig; }
52810:           ranks.set(targetKey + '|' + r.playerKey, rank);
52811:         });
52812:       });
52813:       console.debug('[SQ] Target Points % global rank source used', { source:'v_player_target_points_pct', rows:allRows.length, saved_players_only:!!savedSet });
52814:       return ranks;
52815:     }catch(e){
52816:       console.warn('[SQ] Target Points % global rank source unavailable', { required_view:'v_player_target_points_pct', error:e });
52817:       return new Map();
52818:     }

53899:     function pwCount(el, target, dec, dur){
53900:       if (reduced || !Number.isFinite(target)){ el.textContent = Number(target||0).toFixed(dec); return; }
53901:       var t0 = performance.now();
53902:       (function step(t){
53903:         var p = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / dur);
53904:         el.textContent = (target * (1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3))).toFixed(dec);
53905:         if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
53906:       })(t0);
53907:     }
53908:     function parseLatest(ls){
53909:       try{
53910:         if (!ls) return [];
53911:         var a = Array.isArray(ls) ? ls : (typeof ls === 'string' ? (ls.trim().charAt(0) === '[' ? JSON.parse(ls) : ls.split(/[,;\/]/)) : []);
53912:         return a.map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite).slice(0, 4);
53913:       }catch(_){ return []; }
53914:     }
